Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the dna framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 493. The resulting operator achieves bound O(500/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the dna decomposition. By minimality, the element a^493 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 500-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
